
FUNCTION 'SDT 70045 - Hourly Output' GLOBAL
    IF (WebAutomation.IfWebPageContains.WebPageContainsElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Div \'/70045 - Hourly Output by Checkpoint\'']) THEN
        SET SubflowRetryCount TO 0
        SET MaxSubflowRetries TO 2
        SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''False'''
        LOOP WHILE SubflowRetryCount < MaxSubflowRetries AND DownloadSuccess = $'''False'''
            WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data70045 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
            WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Div \'GeneralShift *\'']) FOR 30
            ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            ON ERROR TimeoutError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            END
            @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Input text \'combobox\''] Text: Shift Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
            ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
            END
            WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Div \'SewingDepartment\'']) FOR 30
            ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            ON ERROR TimeoutError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            END
            @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Input text \'combobox\' 2'] Text: $'''%' '%''' Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
            ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
            END
            WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Div \'Checkpoints\'']) FOR 30
            ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            ON ERROR TimeoutError
                SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
            END
        WebAutomation.ExecuteJavascript BrowserInstance: Browser Javascript: $'''function ExecuteScript() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
        var attempts = 0, maxAttempts = 20, delayMs = 200; // ~4s ceiling

        (function poll() {
            var input = document.querySelector(\'#msCheckpoint_70045\');

            if (!input) {
                if (attempts++ < maxAttempts) { setTimeout(poll, delayMs); return; }
                resolve(\"Error: Input not found after retries.\");
                return;
            }

            // Use Syncfusion\'s own API to open the popup
            if (input.ej2_instances && input.ej2_instances.length > 0) {
                input.ej2_instances[0].showPopup();
                resolve(\"Success: Opened via Syncfusion API.\");
                return;
            }

            // Fallback: dispatch mouse events
            var wrapper = input.closest(\'.e-multi-select-wrapper\');
            if (wrapper) {
                wrapper.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(\'mousedown\', { bubbles: true }));
                wrapper.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(\'mouseup\',  { bubbles: true }));
                wrapper.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(\'click\',    { bubbles: true }));
                resolve(\"Success: Opened via mouse events.\");
                return;
            }

            resolve(\"Error: Could not open dropdown.\");
        })();
    });
}'''
        ON ERROR REPEAT 5 TIMES WAIT 3
        END
        WebAutomation.ExecuteJavascript BrowserInstance: Browser Javascript: $'''function ExecuteScript() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
        var targetValues = [\"SEWING ENDLINE QC\", \"FIN OUT\"];
        var attempts = 0, maxAttempts = 20, delayMs = 200; // ~4s ceiling

        (function poll() {
            var input = document.querySelector(\'#msCheckpoint_70045\');
            var instance = (input && input.ej2_instances && input.ej2_instances.length > 0)
                ? input.ej2_instances[0] : null;

            if (!instance) {
                if (attempts++ < maxAttempts) { setTimeout(poll, delayMs); return; }
                resolve(input ? \"Error: Syncfusion instance not found after retries.\" : \"Error: Input not found after retries.\");
                return;
            }

            var currentValues = instance.value ? Array.from(instance.value) : [];
            var sameSet = currentValues.length === targetValues.length &&
                targetValues.every(function (v) { return currentValues.indexOf(v) !== -1; });

            if (sameSet) {
                resolve(\"Skipped: Already selected.\");
                return;
            }

            instance.value = [];
            instance.value = targetValues;

            resolve(\"Success: \" + instance.value.length + \" selected (\" + instance.value.join(\", \") + \").\");
        })();
    });
}'''
        ON ERROR REPEAT 5 TIMES WAIT 3
        END
        WebAutomation.ExecuteJavascript BrowserInstance: Browser Javascript: $'''function ExecuteScript() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
        var targets = [
            \'[id*=\"chkbIncludeBreakTime\"]\',
            \'[id*=\"chkbShowTargetDetails\"]\',
            \'[id*=\"chkbShowOrderDetails\"]\'
        ];
        var results = [];
        var i = 0;

        function next() {
            if (i >= targets.length) { resolve(results.join(\" | \")); return; }
            var selector = targets[i];
            var attempts = 0, maxAttempts = 20, delayMs = 200; // ~4s ceiling

            (function poll() {
                var input = document.querySelector(selector);
                var wrapper = input && input.closest(\'.e-checkbox-wrapper\');
                var checkSpan = wrapper && wrapper.querySelector(\'span.e-frame\');

                if (checkSpan) {
                    if (!checkSpan.classList.contains(\'e-check\')) {
                        checkSpan.click();
                        results.push(selector + \": Clicked\");
                    } else {
                        results.push(selector + \": Already checked\");
                    }
                    i++; next();
                } else if (attempts++ < maxAttempts) {
                    setTimeout(poll, delayMs);
                } else {
                    results.push(selector + \": Not found after retries\");
                    i++; next();
                }
            })();
        }
        next();
    });
}'''
        ON ERROR REPEAT 5 TIMES WAIT 3
        END
        WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Input text \'datepicker\'']) FOR 30
        ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        ON ERROR TimeoutError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        END
        @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Input text \'datepicker\''] Text: FormattedDateTime Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
        ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
        END
        WAIT 2
        WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Button \'VIEW REPORT\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
        ON ERROR PressButtonError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        END
        LABEL 'Wait for tool ber SDT 70045'
        WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Span \'viewer_toolbar_export\'']) FOR 60
        ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        ON ERROR TimeoutError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        END
        WAIT 5
        WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['List item \'Export\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
        WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['Span \'Excel\'']) FOR 2 
        ON ERROR REPEAT 5 TIMES WAIT 2
        ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
            GOTO 'Wait for tool ber SDT 70045'
        ON ERROR TimeoutError
            GOTO 'Wait for tool ber SDT 70045'
        END
        WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Checkpoint\'']['List item \'Excel Export\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
        WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'70045 -  ...  - Google Chrome\'']['Window \'Save As\'']) FOR 300 
        ON ERROR WaitForWindowContentError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        ON ERROR TimeoutError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        END
        UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'70045 -  ...  - Google Chrome\'']['Edit \'File name:\''] Text: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Hourly Report for 70045.xls''' Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
        UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'70045 -  ...  - Google Chrome\'']['Button \'Save\''] Simulate: False
        ON ERROR PressButtonError
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
            GOTO 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        END
        WAIT (File.WaitForFile.Created File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Hourly Report for 70045.xls''') FOR 120 
        ON ERROR TimeoutError
            GOTO 'Wait for tool ber SDT 70045'
        END
        SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''True'''
        LABEL 'Retry Subflow 70045'
        IF DownloadSuccess = $'''False''' THEN
            WAIT 3
        END
        END
        IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Hourly Report for 70045.xls''') THEN
            Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''❌ %CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Hourly Report for 70045 - Failed after %MaxSubflowRetries% attempts''' List: Missing_Files
        END
    ELSE
        Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''⚠️ %CurrentFactory% - SDT 70045 - Report page not available''' List: Missing_Files
    END
END FUNCTION
